- **referee**: Judges how well a set of explored angles fit together. The blind list (SUITS.md, CLAIMS.md, DECISIONS.md, memory, review_response/, lab docs, git history) is enforced by the PreToolUse hook .claude/hooks/referee-blind.sh, which also confines writes to review_response/referee/. Configures callstack/reassure interaction tests, Flashlight CI integration, bundle size thresholds, and documents baselines. Spawned by perf-control to implement monitoring. This agent CAN write files. ([jeferson-sb/skills](https://github.com/jeferson-sb/skills/blob/HEAD/react-native-perf-skills/agents/regression-testing-specialist.md) · ★3 · 2 copies)
- **regression-tests**: Rules for adding and running tests under `regression/`. The quickstart ctest commands and the 10-minute suite cap live in AGENTS.md (Testing). ([esbmc/esbmc](https://github.com/esbmc/esbmc/blob/HEAD/docs/agents/regression-tests.md) · ★546)
- **regression-theme-monitor**: Monitors theme system: --color-accent-text edge case. Use when: theme audit, contrast theme check, accent color regression scan. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/regression-theme-monitor.agent.md))
- **regression-tracker**: Compares current eval results against run_log.jsonl history to detect regressions, trend shifts, and anomalies. Flags anything that deviates from established baselines. ([GOATnote-Inc/scribegoat2](https://github.com/GOATnote-Inc/scribegoat2/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/regression-tracker.md) · ★4)
- **regression-triage**: Dispatch this agent FIRST whenever a bug, symptom, or failure is reported — before any hypothesis is formed in the main conversation. It searches the durable record (git history, SYMPTOMS.md, DECISIONS.md, code comments, test suite) for the symptom and returns a triage report: known symptom or novel, known causes and their discriminators, what has already been ruled out, and the cheapest next discriminating experiment. ([clyde-tressler/semantic-compactor](https://github.com/clyde-tressler/semantic-compactor/blob/HEAD/agents/regression-triage.md))
- **regression-triage-agent**: You are the **failure classifier** of the regression-results triage pipeline (`/qa-triage-results`, and its future headless twin). For a single **regression issue** — a case that came back `FAIL`, `BLOCKED`, or `SKIPPED`, assembled by `scripts/lib/regression-triage.ts collect` — you decide *why*: is it a real product defect, or is the **test/environment** at fault (bad steps, a stale assertion, dr ([VirtoCommerce/vc-mcp-testing-module](https://github.com/VirtoCommerce/vc-mcp-testing-module/blob/HEAD/ci/agents/regression-triage-agent.md) · ★2)
- **regression-type-hint-monitor**: Monitors type hints on public APIs. Use when: type safety audit, type hint check, public API annotation scan. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/regression-type-hint-monitor.agent.md))
- **regression-url-namespace-monitor**: Monitors URL namespace integrity: app_name conflicts. Use when: URL audit, namespace check, routing conflict scan. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/regression-url-namespace-monitor.agent.md))
- **regression-verifier**: Use after every non-trivial implementation, before code review, to independently verify the change against its acceptance criteria and prove nothing that worked before is broken — baseline and contract tests, test-integrity check, missing edge cases. Never trusts the implementer's own claims. May add tests; never changes production code. ([Beshlee/claude-team-kit](https://github.com/Beshlee/claude-team-kit/blob/HEAD/agents/regression-verifier.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **regression-verifier**: Verifies Regression Watch's confirmed regressions before any alert fires. Use after run_checks.mjs produces findings. Re-reads each confirmed regression against its evidence and the baseline, drops anything unsupported, stale, or explained by an intentional change, and authorizes only the real ones. ([Chykalophia/cklph-marketplace](https://github.com/Chykalophia/cklph-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/cklph-regression-watch/agents/regression-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **regression-verifier**: INTERNAL: Part of /kenspc-task-review orchestration. Requires a RUN_DIR CONTEXT key pointing at the run directory that holds the 5 review reports and code-fixer's Schema B — standalone invocation will fail the prerequisite check. Do not auto-delegate. ([kenspc/kenspc-claude-plugin](https://github.com/kenspc/kenspc-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/kenspc/agents/regression-verifier.md) · ★1)
- **regression-verifier**: Checks a fix for regressions, running the full test suite, opening the touched routes in a browser and explaining every changed line. Dispatched by systematic-debugging (phase 5). ([boparaiamrit/superdev](https://github.com/boparaiamrit/superdev/blob/HEAD/plugins/superdev/agents/regression-verifier.md))
- **regression-verifier**: Use when a shared/common widget, component, class, function, or utility has been modified or replaced. Stack-agnostic (Flutter/Dart, Java/Kotlin/Spring, JS/TS/React, Go, Python, etc.). Run this after committing the changes. Scans all repos for usages, reads old implementation from git history, diffs old vs new behaviour, and produces a regression report. Invoke as: "run regression-verifier. Old: &lt;OldName(s)&gt;. New: &lt;NewName&gt;. Repos: &lt;paths&gt; ([scapia-oss/compass](https://github.com/scapia-oss/compass/blob/HEAD/tools/cc-sdd/templates/plugin/agents/regression-verifier.md))
- **regression-verifier**: Flutter(Dart) 코드 변경 후 회귀(regression)가 발생했는지 검증할 때 사용. flutter analyze와 관련 테스트(위젯·골든·통합 포함)를 실행하고 diff를 분석해 기존 UI·동작이 유지되는지 확인한다. 변경을 완료로 간주하기 전에 반드시(use proactively) 호출할 것. ([timinguniq/sedae_buget](https://github.com/timinguniq/sedae_buget/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/regression-verifier.md))
- **regression-waf-rate-limit-monitor**: Monitors WAF rate limits: RateLimitRule config. Use when: WAF audit, rate limit check, IP blocking scan. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/regression-waf-rate-limit-monitor.agent.md))
- **regression-xss-monitor**: Monitors for XSS regression: removed sanitization, |safe without source. Use when: XSS audit, sanitization check, template safe filter scan. ([engremran07/gsmvault](https://github.com/engremran07/gsmvault/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/regression-xss-monitor.agent.md))
- **regression_tester**: Run cross-deliverable regression tests ([BechtelR/AutoBuilder](https://github.com/BechtelR/AutoBuilder/blob/HEAD/app/agents/regression_tester.md))
- **RegressionDetective**: Investigates regressions — bisects between known-good and known-bad, identifies offending commit, recommends fix. Use proactively when something "used to work", a performance regression shows in CI, a test flips green to red, or behavior changed after a deploy. ([jokerman89/lintel](https://github.com/jokerman89/lintel/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/RegressionDetective.agent.md) · ★1)
- **regtest-test-writer**: Use when writing or extending tests for go-regtest — happy-path coverage for a new method, error/validation paths, or concurrency/lifecycle tests. Follows the conventions in AGENTS.md ("Tests"). ([neverDefined/go-regtest](https://github.com/neverDefined/go-regtest/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/regtest-test-writer.md) · ★1)
